package za.co.sprinta.app.ads

import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Context
import com.google.android.gms.ads.AdRequest
import com.google.android.gms.ads.LoadAdError
import com.google.android.gms.ads.MobileAds
import com.google.android.gms.ads.interstitial.InterstitialAd
import com.google.android.gms.ads.interstitial.InterstitialAdLoadCallback
import com.google.android.ump.UserMessagingPlatform

object AdManager {
    const val BANNER_AD_UNIT_ID = "ca-app-pub-5833611172742289/3073358578"
    const val INTERSTITIAL_AD_UNIT_ID = "ca-app-pub-5833611172742289/4286365295"

    private var interstitialAd: InterstitialAd? = null
    private var isInitializing = false

    fun gatherConsentAndInitialize(activity: Activity) {
        if (isInitializing) return
        isInitializing = true

        val consentInformation = UserMessagingPlatform.getConsentInformation(activity)
        val params = com.google.android.ump.ConsentRequestParameters.Builder().build()

        consentInformation.requestConsentInfoUpdate(
            activity,
            params,
            {
                UserMessagingPlatform.loadAndShowConsentFormIfRequired(activity) { _ ->
                    initMobileAds(activity.applicationContext)
                }
            },
            { _ ->
                initMobileAds(activity.applicationContext)
            }
        )
    }

    private fun initMobileAds(context: Context) {
        MobileAds.initialize(context) {
            preloadInterstitial(context)
        }
    }

    fun preloadInterstitial(context: Context) {
        val adRequest = AdRequest.Builder().build()
        InterstitialAd.load(
            context,
            INTERSTITIAL_AD_UNIT_ID,
            adRequest,
            object : InterstitialAdLoadCallback() {
                override fun onAdLoaded(ad: InterstitialAd) {
                    interstitialAd = ad
                }

                override fun onAdFailedToLoad(error: LoadAdError) {
                    interstitialAd = null
                }
            }
        )
    }

    fun showInterstitialIfReady(activity: Activity, onAdDismissed: () -> Unit) {
        val ad = interstitialAd
        if (ad != null) {
            ad.fullScreenContentCallback = object : com.google.android.gms.ads.FullScreenContentCallback() {
                override fun onAdDismissedFullScreenContent() {
                    interstitialAd = null
                    preloadInterstitial(activity.applicationContext)
                    onAdDismissed()
                }

                override fun onAdFailedToShowFullScreenContent(p0: com.google.android.gms.ads.AdError) {
                    interstitialAd = null
                    onAdDismissed()
                }
            }
            ad.show(activity)
        } else {
            onAdDismissed()
        }
    }
}

fun showInterstitialAd(
    context: Context,
    isPremium: Boolean,
    onAdDismissed: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    if (isPremium) {
        onAdDismissed()
        return
    }

    val activity = context as? android.app.Activity
    if (activity == null) {
        onAdDismissed()
        return
    }

    val adRequest = AdRequest.Builder().build()
    InterstitialAd.load(
        context,
        AdUnitIds.INTERSTITIAL,
        adRequest,
        object : InterstitialAdLoadCallback() {
            override fun onAdLoaded(interstitialAd: InterstitialAd) {
                interstitialAd.show(activity)
                onAdDismissed()
            }

            override fun onAdFailedToLoad(error: LoadAdError) {
                onAdDismissed()
            }
        }
    )
}

fun showRewardedAd(
    context: Context,
    isPremium: Boolean,
    onRewardEarned: () -> Unit
) {
    if (isPremium) {
        onRewardEarned()
        return
    }

    val activity = context as? android.app.Activity
    if (activity == null) {
        onRewardEarned()
        return
    }

    val adRequest = AdRequest.Builder().build()
    RewardedInterstitialAd.load(
        context,
        AdUnitIds.REWARDED_INTERSTITIAL,
        adRequest,
        object : RewardedInterstitialAdLoadCallback() {
            override fun onAdLoaded(rewardedAd: RewardedInterstitialAd) {
                rewardedAd.show(activity) {
                    onRewardEarned()
                }
            }

            override fun onAdFailedToLoad(error: LoadAdError) {
                onRewardEarned()
            }
        }
    )
}


=== C:\Users\dskip\Downloads\SPRINTA_POE_PART2_SOURCE\SPRINTA_POE_PART2\app\src\main\java\za\co\sprinta\app\billing\BillingManager.kt ===

package za.co.sprinta.app.billing

import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Context
import com.android.billingclient.api.AcknowledgePurchaseParams
import com.android.billingclient.api.BillingClient
import com.android.billingclient.api.BillingClientStateListener
import com.android.billingclient.api.BillingFlowParams
import com.android.billingclient.api.BillingResult
import com.android.billingclient.api.ProductDetails
import com.android.billingclient.api.Purchase
import com.android.billingclient.api.PurchasesUpdatedListener
import com.android.billingclient.api.QueryProductDetailsParams
import com.android.billingclient.api.QueryPurchasesParams
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow

class BillingManager(
    context: Context,
    private val onPremiumStatusChanged: (Boolean) -> Unit
) : PurchasesUpdatedListener {

    private val billingClient = BillingClient.newBuilder(context)
        .setListener(this)
        .enablePendingPurchases()
        .build()

    private val _proProductDetails = MutableStateFlow<ProductDetails?>(null)
    val proProductDetails: StateFlow<ProductDetails?> = _proProductDetails

    init {
        startConnection()
    }

    private fun startConnection() {
        billingClient.startConnection(object : BillingClientStateListener {
            override fun onBillingSetupFinished(result: BillingResult) {
                if (result.responseCode == BillingClient.BillingResponseCode.OK) {
                    queryProducts()
                    queryActivePurchases()
                }
            }

            override fun onBillingServiceDisconnected() {
            }
        })
    }

    private fun queryProducts() {
        val params = QueryProductDetailsParams.newBuilder()
            .setProductList(
                listOf(
                    QueryProductDetailsParams.Product.newBuilder()
                        .setProductId("sprinta_pro_monthly")
                        .setProductType(BillingClient.ProductType.SUBS)
                        .build()
                )
            )
            .build()

        billingClient.queryProductDetailsAsync(params) { _, productDetailsList ->
            if (productDetailsList.isNotEmpty()) {
                _proProductDetails.value = productDetailsList[0]
            }
        }
    }

    fun launchBillingFlow(activity: Activity) {
        val details = _proProductDetails.value ?: return
        val offerToken = details.subscriptionOfferDetails?.firstOrNull()?.offerToken ?: return

        val paramsList = listOf(
            BillingFlowParams.ProductDetailsParams.newBuilder()
                .setProductDetails(details)
                .setOfferToken(offerToken)
                .build()
        )

        val flowParams = BillingFlowParams.newBuilder()
            .setProductDetailsParamsList(paramsList)
            .build()

        billingClient.launchBillingFlow(activity, flowParams)
    }

    override fun onPurchasesUpdated(result: BillingResult, purchases: MutableList<Purchase>?) {
        if (result.responseCode == BillingClient.BillingResponseCode.OK && purchases != null) {
            for (p in purchases) {
                handlePurchase(p)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun handlePurchase(purchase: Purchase) {
        if (purchase.purchaseState == Purchase.PurchaseState.PURCHASED) {
            if (!purchase.isAcknowledged) {
                val acknowledgeParams = AcknowledgePurchaseParams.newBuilder()
                    .setPurchaseToken(purchase.purchaseToken)
                    .build()
                billingClient.acknowledgePurchase(acknowledgeParams) { res ->
                    if (res.responseCode == BillingClient.BillingResponseCode.OK) {
                        onPremiumStatusChanged(true)
                    }
                }
            } else {
                onPremiumStatusChanged(true)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun queryActivePurchases() {
        val params = QueryPurchasesParams.newBuilder()
            .setProductType(BillingClient.ProductType.SUBS)
            .build()

        billingClient.queryPurchasesAsync(params) { _, purchases ->
            val isPro = purchases.any { it.purchaseState == Purchase.PurchaseState.PURCHASED }
            onPremiumStatusChanged(isPro)
        }
    }
}


=== C:\Users\dskip\Downloads\SPRINTA_POE_PART2_SOURCE\SPRINTA_POE_PART2\app\src\main\java\za\co\sprinta\app\billing\SprintaBillingViewModel.kt ===

package za.co.sprinta.app.billing

import android.app.Activity
import android.app.Application
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import com.android.billingclient.api.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow

class SprintaBillingViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application), PurchasesUpdatedListener {

    private val billingClient = BillingClient.newBuilder(application)
        .setListener(this)
        .enablePendingPurchases()
        .build()

    private val _isProUser = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val isProUser: StateFlow<Boolean> = _isProUser

    private val _proProductDetails = MutableStateFlow<ProductDetails?>(null)
    val proProductDetails: StateFlow<ProductDetails?> = _proProductDetails

    init {
        connectToPlayBilling()
    }

    private fun connectToPlayBilling() {
        billingClient.startConnection(object : BillingClientStateListener {
            override fun onBillingSetupFinished(billingResult: BillingResult) {
                if (billingResult.responseCode == BillingClient.BillingResponseCode.OK) {
                    querySubscriptionProducts()
                    checkExistingPurchases()
                }
            }
            override fun onBillingServiceDisconnected() {
                // Retry connection logic here in production
            }
        })
    }

    private fun querySubscriptionProducts() {
        val queryProductDetailsParams = QueryProductDetailsParams.newBuilder()
            .setProductList(
                listOf(
                    QueryProductDetailsParams.Product.newBuilder()
                        .setProductId("sprinta_pro_monthly") // This must match your Google Play Console ID
                        .setProductType(BillingClient.ProductType.SUBS)
                        .build()
                )
            )
            .build()

        billingClient.queryProductDetailsAsync(queryProductDetailsParams) { _, productDetailsList ->
            if (productDetailsList.isNotEmpty()) {
                _proProductDetails.value = productDetailsList[0]
            }
        }
    }

    fun launchBillingFlow(activity: Activity) {
        val productDetails = _proProductDetails.value ?: return
        val offerToken = productDetails.subscriptionOfferDetails?.get(0)?.offerToken ?: return

        val productDetailsParamsList = listOf(
            BillingFlowParams.ProductDetailsParams.newBuilder()
                .setProductDetails(productDetails)
                .setOfferToken(offerToken)
                .build()
        )

        val billingFlowParams = BillingFlowParams.newBuilder()
            .setProductDetailsParamsList(productDetailsParamsList)
            .build()

        billingClient.launchBillingFlow(activity, billingFlowParams)
    }

    override fun onPurchasesUpdated(billingResult: BillingResult, purchases: MutableList<Purchase>?) {
        if (billingResult.responseCode == BillingClient.BillingResponseCode.OK && purchases != null) {
            for (purchase in purchases) {
                acknowledgePurchase(purchase)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun acknowledgePurchase(purchase: Purchase) {
        if (purchase.purchaseState == Purchase.PurchaseState.PURCHASED) {
            if (!purchase.isAcknowledged) {
                val acknowledgePurchaseParams = AcknowledgePurchaseParams.newBuilder()
                    .setPurchaseToken(purchase.purchaseToken)
                    .build()
                billingClient.acknowledgePurchase(acknowledgePurchaseParams) { billingResult ->
                    if (billingResult.responseCode == BillingClient.BillingResponseCode.OK) {
                        _isProUser.value = true
                    }
                }
            } else {
                _isProUser.value = true
            }
        }
    }

    private fun checkExistingPurchases() {
        billingClient.queryPurchasesAsync(
            QueryPurchasesParams.newBuilder().setProductType(BillingClient.ProductType.SUBS).build()
        ) { _, purchases ->
            val hasActiveSub = purchases.any { it.purchaseState == Purchase.PurchaseState.PURCHASED }
            _isProUser.value = hasActiveSub
        }
    }
}

package za.co.sprinta.app.data.local

import androidx.room.Dao
import androidx.room.Database
import androidx.room.Entity
import androidx.room.Insert
import androidx.room.OnConflictStrategy
import androidx.room.PrimaryKey
import androidx.room.Query
import androidx.room.RoomDatabase
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow

@Entity(tableName = "workouts")
data class WorkoutEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val workoutId: String,
    val userId: String,
    val distanceMeters: Double,
    val durationSeconds: Long,
    val averagePaceSecondsPerKm: Double,
    val startTimeIso: String,
    val totalElevation: Double,
    val notes: String,
    val routePointsJson: String,
    val isSynced: Boolean = false,
    val activityType: String = "RUN"
)

@Entity(tableName = "personal_records")
data class PersonalRecordEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val key: String,
    val userId: String,
    val recordType: String,
    val valueSecondsPerKm: Double,
    val workoutId: String,
    val achievedAtIso: String
)

@Dao
@JvmSuppressWildcards
interface WorkoutDao {
    @Query("SELECT * FROM workouts WHERE userId = :userId ORDER BY startTimeIso DESC")
    fun observeWorkouts(userId: String): Flow<List<WorkoutEntity>>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM workouts WHERE isSynced = 0 ORDER BY startTimeIso ASC")
    suspend fun getUnsynced(): List<WorkoutEntity>

    @Query("UPDATE workouts SET isSynced = 1 WHERE workoutId = :workoutId")
    suspend fun markSynced(workoutId: String): Int

    @Query("SELECT * FROM workouts WHERE userId = :userId ORDER BY startTimeIso DESC")
    suspend fun getWorkouts(userId: String): List<WorkoutEntity>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertWorkout(workout: WorkoutEntity): Long
}

@Dao
@JvmSuppressWildcards
interface PersonalRecordDao {
    @Query("SELECT * FROM personal_records WHERE userId = :userId")
    suspend fun getRecords(userId: String): List<PersonalRecordEntity>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertRecord(record: PersonalRecordEntity): Long
}

@Database(
    entities = [WorkoutEntity::class, PersonalRecordEntity::class],
    version = 2,
    exportSchema = false
)
abstract class SprintaDatabase : RoomDatabase() {
    abstract fun workoutDao(): WorkoutDao
    abstract fun personalRecordDao(): PersonalRecordDao
}

import za.co.sprinta.app.model.WorkoutDto

interface SprintaApiService {
    @POST("api/v1/auth/register")
    suspend fun register(@Body request: RegisterRequest): Response<AuthResponse>

    @POST("api/v1/auth/login")
    suspend fun login(@Body request: LoginRequest): Response<AuthResponse>

    @POST("api/v1/runs/sync")
    suspend fun syncWorkout(
        @Header("Authorization") token: String,
        @Body workout: WorkoutDto
    ): Response<SyncResponse>

    @GET("api/v1/runs/user/{userId}")
    suspend fun getUserWorkouts(
        @Header("Authorization") token: String,
        @Path("userId") userId: String
    ): Response<List<WorkoutDto>>
}

package za.co.sprinta.app.data.repository

import com.google.gson.Gson
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import retrofit2.Response
import za.co.sprinta.app.data.local.PersonalRecordEntity
import za.co.sprinta.app.data.local.SprintaDatabase
import za.co.sprinta.app.data.local.WorkoutEntity
import za.co.sprinta.app.data.remote.SprintaApiService
import za.co.sprinta.app.model.AuthResponse
import za.co.sprinta.app.model.LoginRequest
import za.co.sprinta.app.model.RegisterRequest
import za.co.sprinta.app.model.RoutePoint
import za.co.sprinta.app.model.WorkoutDto
import za.co.sprinta.app.prefs.SecureSessionStore
import za.co.sprinta.app.util.PersonalRecordUtils

class SprintaRepository(
    private val database: SprintaDatabase,
    private val api: SprintaApiService,
    private val session: SecureSessionStore
) {
    private val gson = Gson()

    fun observeWorkouts(userId: String): Flow<List<WorkoutEntity>> =
        database.workoutDao().observeWorkouts(userId)

    suspend fun register(name: String, email: String, password: String): Result<AuthResponse> =
        executeAuth { api.register(RegisterRequest(name.trim(), email.trim(), password)) }

    suspend fun login(email: String, password: String): Result<AuthResponse> =
        executeAuth { api.login(LoginRequest(email.trim(), password)) }

    private suspend fun executeAuth(call: suspend () -> Response<AuthResponse>): Result<AuthResponse> {
        return try {
            val response = call()
            if (response.isSuccessful && response.body()?.user != null) {
                Result.success(response.body()!!)
            } else {
                Result.failure(IllegalStateException("Authentication request failed: ${response.code()}"))
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }

    fun saveSession(response: AuthResponse) {
        val user = requireNotNull(response.user)
        session.save(user.id, user.name, user.email, response.token)
    }

    fun saveUserSession(id: String, name: String, email: String, token: String = "firebase_token") {
        session.save(id, name, email, token)
    }

    fun clearSession() = session.clear()

    fun currentSession() = session.load()

    suspend fun saveWorkout(workout: WorkoutEntity): List<String> {
        database.workoutDao().insertWorkout(workout)
        val existing = database.personalRecordDao().getRecords(workout.userId)
        val newRecords = PersonalRecordUtils.detectNewRecords(workout, existing)
        newRecords.forEach { record ->
            database.personalRecordDao().insertRecord(
                PersonalRecordUtils.toEntity(record, workout.userId, workout.workoutId)
            )
        }
        return newRecords.map { it.type }
    }

    suspend fun getUnsyncedWorkouts(): List<WorkoutEntity> = database.workoutDao().getUnsynced()

    suspend fun markSynced(workoutId: String) = database.workoutDao().markSynced(workoutId)

    suspend fun refreshFromServer(): Result<Int> {
        val current = session.load() ?: return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("No session"))
        return try {
            val response = api.getUserWorkouts(
                "Bearer ${current["token"]}",
                current["id"]!!
            )
            if (!response.isSuccessful) {
                Result.failure(IllegalStateException("History request failed: ${response.code()}"))
            } else {
                val items = response.body().orEmpty()
                items.forEach { dto ->
                    val entity = WorkoutEntity(
                        workoutId = dto.workoutId,
                        userId = dto.userId,
                        distanceMeters = dto.distanceMeters,
                        durationSeconds = dto.durationSeconds,
                        averagePaceSecondsPerKm = dto.averagePaceSecondsPerKm,
                        startTimeIso = dto.startTimeIso,
                        totalElevation = dto.totalElevation,
                        notes = dto.notes,
                        routePointsJson = gson.toJson(dto.routePoints),
                        isSynced = true
                    )
                    database.workoutDao().insertWorkout(entity)
                }
                Result.success(items.size)
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }

    fun entityToDto(entity: WorkoutEntity): WorkoutDto {
        val points: List<RoutePoint> = runCatching {
            gson.fromJson(entity.routePointsJson, Array<RoutePoint>::class.java)?.toList() ?: emptyList()
        }.getOrDefault(emptyList())

        return WorkoutDto(
            workoutId = entity.workoutId,
            userId = entity.userId,
            distanceMeters = entity.distanceMeters,
            durationSeconds = entity.durationSeconds,
            averagePaceSecondsPerKm = entity.averagePaceSecondsPerKm,
            startTimeIso = entity.startTimeIso,
            totalElevation = entity.totalElevation,
            notes = entity.notes,
            routePoints = points.map {
                za.co.sprinta.app.model.LocationPointDto(
                    it.latitude,
                    it.longitude,
                    it.altitude,
                    it.timestamp
                )
            }
        )
    }
}


=== C:\Users\dskip\Downloads\SPRINTA_POE_PART2_SOURCE\SPRINTA_POE_PART2\app\src\main\java\za\co\sprinta\app\data\sync\SyncWorker.kt ===

package za.co.sprinta.app.data.sync

import android.content.Context
import androidx.work.Constraints
import androidx.work.CoroutineWorker
import androidx.work.NetworkType
import androidx.work.OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder
import androidx.work.WorkManager
import androidx.work.WorkerParameters
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import za.co.sprinta.app.AppGraph

class SyncWorker(
    appContext: Context,
    workerParams: WorkerParameters
) : CoroutineWorker(appContext, workerParams) {

    override suspend fun doWork(): Result = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val unsynced = AppGraph.repository.getUnsyncedWorkouts()
        if (unsynced.isEmpty()) return@withContext Result.success()

        val token = AppGraph.session.load()?.get("token")

        return@withContext try {
            for (workout in unsynced) {
                if (token != null) {
                    val response = AppGraph.api.syncWorkout(
                        "Bearer $token",
                        AppGraph.repository.entityToDto(workout)
                    )
                    if (response.isSuccessful) {
                        AppGraph.repository.markSynced(workout.workoutId)
                    } else if (response.code() in 500..599) {
                        return@withContext Result.retry()
                    }
                } else {
                    AppGraph.repository.markSynced(workout.workoutId)
                }
            }
            Result.success()
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            for (workout in unsynced) {
                AppGraph.repository.markSynced(workout.workoutId)
            }
            Result.success()
        }
    }

    companion object {
        fun schedule(context: Context) {
            val constraints = Constraints.Builder()
                .setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED)
                .build()

            val request = OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder<SyncWorker>()
                .setConstraints(constraints)
                .build()

            WorkManager.getInstance(context).enqueue(request)
        }
    }
}

package za.co.sprinta.app.prefs

import android.content.Context
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.booleanPreferencesKey
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.edit
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.intPreferencesKey
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.stringPreferencesKey
import androidx.datastore.preferences.preferencesDataStore
import androidx.security.crypto.EncryptedSharedPreferences
import androidx.security.crypto.MasterKey
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map

private val Context.dataStore by preferencesDataStore(name = "sprinta_settings")

data class AppPreferences(
    val darkMode: Boolean = false,
    val useMiles: Boolean = false,
    val audioIntervalKm: Int = 1,
    val language: String = "en"
)

class PreferencesStore(private val context: Context) {
    private val darkKey = booleanPreferencesKey("dark_mode")
    private val milesKey = booleanPreferencesKey("use_miles")
    private val audioKey = intPreferencesKey("audio_interval")
    private val languageKey = stringPreferencesKey("language")

    val preferences: Flow<AppPreferences> = context.dataStore.data.map { p ->
        AppPreferences(
            darkMode = p[darkKey] ?: false,
            useMiles = p[milesKey] ?: false,
            audioIntervalKm = p[audioKey] ?: 1,
            language = p[languageKey] ?: "en"
        )
    }

    suspend fun setDarkMode(value: Boolean) = context.dataStore.edit { it[darkKey] = value }
    suspend fun setUseMiles(value: Boolean) = context.dataStore.edit { it[milesKey] = value }
    suspend fun setAudioInterval(value: Int) = context.dataStore.edit { it[audioKey] = value }
    suspend fun setLanguage(value: String) = context.dataStore.edit { it[languageKey] = value }
}

class SecureSessionStore(context: Context) {
    private val prefs = run {
        val masterKey = MasterKey.Builder(context)
            .setKeyScheme(MasterKey.KeyScheme.AES256_GCM)
            .build()
        EncryptedSharedPreferences.create(
            context,
            "sprinta_secure_session",
            masterKey,
            EncryptedSharedPreferences.PrefKeyEncryptionScheme.AES256_SIV,
            EncryptedSharedPreferences.PrefValueEncryptionScheme.AES256_GCM
        )
    }

    fun save(id: String, name: String, email: String, token: String) {
        prefs.edit()
            .putString("id", id)
            .putString("name", name)
            .putString("email", email)
            .putString("token", token)
            .apply()
    }

    fun load(): Map<String, String>? {
        val id = prefs.getString("id", null) ?: return null
        val name = prefs.getString("name", null) ?: return null
        val email = prefs.getString("email", null) ?: return null
        val token = prefs.getString("token", null) ?: return null
        return mapOf("id" to id, "name" to name, "email" to email, "token" to token)
    }

    fun clear() = prefs.edit().clear().apply()
}


=== C:\Users\dskip\Downloads\SPRINTA_POE_PART2_SOURCE\SPRINTA_POE_PART2\app\src\main\java\za\co\sprinta\app\service\LocationTrackingService.kt ===

package za.co.sprinta.app.service

import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.Service
import android.content.Intent
import android.location.Location
import android.os.Build
import android.os.IBinder
import android.os.Looper
import android.speech.tts.TextToSpeech
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import com.google.android.gms.location.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.*
import za.co.sprinta.app.AppGraph
import za.co.sprinta.app.R
import za.co.sprinta.app.tracking.TrackingStore
import za.co.sprinta.app.util.GeoUtils
import java.util.Locale

class LocationTrackingService : Service(), TextToSpeech.OnInitListener {
    private lateinit var fusedLocationClient: FusedLocationProviderClient
    private var tts: TextToSpeech? = null

    private val serviceScope = CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.IO + SupervisorJob())
    private var audioIntervalKm: Int = 1
    private var lastAnnouncedKm: Int = 0

    private val callback = object : LocationCallback() {
        override fun onLocationResult(result: LocationResult) {
            result.locations.forEach { location: Location ->
                // GPS Jitter Filter: Ignore accuracy worse than 15 meters
                if (location.accuracy > 15f) return@forEach

                val currentPoints = TrackingStore.state.value.points
                val previous = currentPoints.lastOrNull()

                if (previous != null) {
                    val timeDiffSeconds = (location.time - previous.timestamp) / 1000.0
                    val dist = GeoUtils.haversineMeters(
                        previous.latitude, previous.longitude,
                        location.latitude, location.longitude
                    )
                    // Speed Filter: Ignore impossible human speeds (> 15 meters/sec / 54 km/h)
                    if (timeDiffSeconds > 0 && (dist / timeDiffSeconds) > 15.0) return@forEach
                }

                TrackingStore.addLocation(location)
                checkAndAnnounceSplit()
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        fusedLocationClient = LocationServices.getFusedLocationProviderClient(this)
        tts = TextToSpeech(this, this)

        // Observe user settings for Audio Updates
        serviceScope.launch {
            AppGraph.preferences.preferences.collect { prefs ->
                audioIntervalKm = prefs.audioIntervalKm
            }
        }

        createNotificationChannel()
        startTrackingForeground()
    }

    private fun checkAndAnnounceSplit() {
        if (audioIntervalKm == 0) return // Audio updates turned off in settings

        val currentKm = (TrackingStore.state.value.distanceMeters / 1000).toInt()
        if (currentKm >= lastAnnouncedKm + audioIntervalKm) {
            lastAnnouncedKm = currentKm
            val pace = GeoUtils.formatPace(TrackingStore.state.value.paceSecondsPerKm)

            val message = "$currentKm kilometer${if (currentKm > 1) "s" else ""} completed. Average pace: $pace."
            tts?.speak(message, TextToSpeech.QUEUE_FLUSH, null, null)
        }
    }

    override fun onInit(status: Int) {
        if (status == TextToSpeech.SUCCESS) {
            tts?.language = Locale.getDefault()
        }
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        when (intent?.action) {
            ACTION_START -> startUpdates()
            ACTION_STOP -> stopTracking()
        }
        return START_STICKY
    }

    private fun startTrackingForeground() {
        val notification = NotificationCompat.Builder(this, CHANNEL_ID)
            .setContentTitle("SPRINTA")
            .setContentText("Tracking your run in real-time...")
            .setSmallIcon(android.R.drawable.ic_media_play)
            .setOngoing(true)
            .build()

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
            startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification, android.content.pm.ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_LOCATION)
        } else {
            startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification)
        }
    }

    private fun startUpdates() {
        TrackingStore.start()
        lastAnnouncedKm = 0

        // Battery Saver Location Request
        val request = LocationRequest.Builder(Priority.PRIORITY_HIGH_ACCURACY, 2000L)
            .setMinUpdateIntervalMillis(1000L)
            .setMinUpdateDistanceMeters(2.0f) // Only fetch if user moved 2 meters
            .build()

        try {
            fusedLocationClient.requestLocationUpdates(request, callback, Looper.getMainLooper())
        } catch (e: SecurityException) {
            stopSelf()
        }
    }

    private fun stopTracking() {
        fusedLocationClient.removeLocationUpdates(callback)
        TrackingStore.stop()
        stopForeground(STOP_FOREGROUND_REMOVE)
        stopSelf()
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        serviceScope.cancel()
        tts?.stop()
        tts?.shutdown()
        fusedLocationClient.removeLocationUpdates(callback)
        super.onDestroy()
    }

    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder? = null

    private fun createNotificationChannel() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val channel = NotificationChannel(CHANNEL_ID, "SPRINTA Run Tracking", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW)
            getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java).createNotificationChannel(channel)
        }
    }

    companion object {
        const val ACTION_START = "za.co.sprinta.app.action.START_TRACKING"
        const val ACTION_STOP = "za.co.sprinta.app.action.STOP_TRACKING"
        private const val CHANNEL_ID = "sprinta_tracking"
        private const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 5101
    }
}


=== C:\Users\dskip\Downloads\SPRINTA_POE_PART2_SOURCE\SPRINTA_POE_PART2\app\src\main\java\za\co\sprinta\app\service\TrackingService.kt ===

package za.co.sprinta.app.service

import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.Service
import android.content.Intent
import android.location.Location
import android.os.Build
import android.os.IBinder
import android.os.Looper
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import com.google.android.gms.location.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import za.co.sprinta.app.tracking.TrackingStore
import za.co.sprinta.app.util.LocationUtils

data class TrackingState(
    val isTracking: Boolean = false,
    val distanceMeters: Double = 0.0,
    val durationSeconds: Long = 0L,
    val currentPaceSecondsPerKm: Double = 0.0,
    val routeCoordinates: List<Pair<Double, Double>> = emptyList()
)

class TrackingService : Service() {

    private lateinit var fusedLocationClient: FusedLocationProviderClient
    private var lastLocation: Location? = null

    private val callback = object : LocationCallback() {
        override fun onLocationResult(result: LocationResult) {
            result.locations.forEach { location ->
                if (LocationUtils.isReliableLocation(location, lastLocation)) {
                    lastLocation = location
                    TrackingStore.addLocation(location)
                    updateTrackingState()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        fusedLocationClient = LocationServices.getFusedLocationProviderClient(this)
        createNotificationChannel()
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        when (intent?.action) {
            ACTION_START -> startTracking()
            ACTION_STOP -> stopTracking()
        }
        return START_STICKY
    }

    private fun startTracking() {
        startForegroundServiceNotification()
        TrackingStore.start()
        updateTrackingState()

        val locationRequest = LocationRequest.Builder(Priority.PRIORITY_HIGH_ACCURACY, 2000L)
            .setMinUpdateIntervalMillis(1000L)
            .setMinUpdateDistanceMeters(2.0f)
            .build()

        try {
            fusedLocationClient.requestLocationUpdates(locationRequest, callback, Looper.getMainLooper())
        } catch (e: SecurityException) {
            stopSelf()
        }
    }

    private fun stopTracking() {
        fusedLocationClient.removeLocationUpdates(callback)
        TrackingStore.stop()
        updateTrackingState()
        stopForeground(STOP_FOREGROUND_REMOVE)
        stopSelf()
    }

    private fun updateTrackingState() {
        val storeState = TrackingStore.state.value
        val coords = storeState.points.map { Pair(it.latitude, it.longitude) }
        _trackingState.value = TrackingState(
            isTracking = storeState.active,
            distanceMeters = storeState.distanceMeters,
            durationSeconds = storeState.durationSeconds,
            currentPaceSecondsPerKm = storeState.paceSecondsPerKm,
            routeCoordinates = coords
        )
    }

    private fun startForegroundServiceNotification() {
        val notification = NotificationCompat.Builder(this, CHANNEL_ID)
            .setContentTitle("SPRINTA Tracking Active")
            .setContentText("Recording your GPS route and live metrics...")
            .setSmallIcon(android.R.drawable.ic_media_play)
            .setOngoing(true)
            .build()

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
            startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification, android.content.pm.ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_LOCATION)
        } else {
            startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification)
        }
    }

    private fun createNotificationChannel() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val channel = NotificationChannel(CHANNEL_ID, "SPRINTA Tracking Engine", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW)
            getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java).createNotificationChannel(channel)
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        fusedLocationClient.removeLocationUpdates(callback)
        super.onDestroy()
    }

    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder? = null

    companion object {
        const val ACTION_START = "za.co.sprinta.app.action.START_TRACKING"
        const val ACTION_STOP = "za.co.sprinta.app.action.STOP_TRACKING"
        private const val CHANNEL_ID = "sprinta_tracking_engine"
        private const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 5102

        private val _trackingState = MutableStateFlow(TrackingState())
        val trackingState: StateFlow<TrackingState> = _trackingState
    }
}


=== C:\Users\dskip\Downloads\SPRINTA_POE_PART2_SOURCE\SPRINTA_POE_PART2\app\src\main\java\za\co\sprinta\app\tracking\TrackingStore.kt ===

package za.co.sprinta.app.tracking

import android.location.Location
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import za.co.sprinta.app.model.RoutePoint
import za.co.sprinta.app.util.GeoUtils

object TrackingStore {
    data class State(
        val active: Boolean = false,
        val startTimeMillis: Long = 0L,
        val distanceMeters: Double = 0.0,
        val durationSeconds: Long = 0L,
        val paceSecondsPerKm: Double = 0.0,
        val totalElevation: Double = 0.0,
        val points: List<RoutePoint> = emptyList()
    )

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(State())
    val state: StateFlow<State> = _state

    fun start() {
        _state.value = State(
            active = true,
            startTimeMillis = System.currentTimeMillis()
        )
    }

    fun addLocation(location: Location) {
        val current = _state.value
        if (!current.active) return

        val newPoint = RoutePoint(
            latitude = location.latitude,
            longitude = location.longitude,
            altitude = location.altitude,
            timestamp = location.time
        )

        val previous = current.points.lastOrNull()
        val incrementalDistance = previous?.let {
            GeoUtils.haversineMeters(
                it.latitude,
                it.longitude,
                newPoint.latitude,
                newPoint.longitude
            )
        } ?: 0.0

        val elevationGain = previous?.let {
            maxOf(0.0, newPoint.altitude - it.altitude)
        } ?: 0.0

        val elapsed = ((System.currentTimeMillis() - current.startTimeMillis) / 1000L).coerceAtLeast(0L)
        val distance = current.distanceMeters + incrementalDistance
        val pace = GeoUtils.paceSecondsPerKm(distance, elapsed)

        _state.value = current.copy(
            distanceMeters = distance,
            durationSeconds = elapsed,
            paceSecondsPerKm = pace,
            totalElevation = current.totalElevation + elevationGain,
            points = current.points + newPoint
        )
    }

    fun stop() {
        val current = _state.value
        val elapsed = ((System.currentTimeMillis() - current.startTimeMillis) / 1000L).coerceAtLeast(current.durationSeconds)
        _state.value = current.copy(
            active = false,
            durationSeconds = elapsed,
            paceSecondsPerKm = GeoUtils.paceSecondsPerKm(current.distanceMeters, elapsed)
        )
    }

    fun reset() {
        _state.value = State()
    }
}

@Composable
fun ManualEntryDialog(
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    onSave: (activityType: String, distanceKm: Double, durationMins: Long, dateIso: String, notes: String) -> Unit
) {
    var selectedActivity by remember { mutableStateOf("RUN") }
    var expandedDropdown by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    var distanceText by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var hoursText by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var minsText by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var notesText by remember { mutableStateOf("") }

    val activityOptions = listOf(
        "RUN" to "ðŸƒâ€â™‚ï¸ Run",
        "WALK" to "ðŸš¶â€â™‚ï¸ Walk",
        "WORKOUT" to "ðŸ‹ï¸â€â™‚ï¸ Workout"
    )

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Text(
                "Manual Workout Entry",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground
            )
        },
        text = {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
            ) {
                Text(
                    "ACTIVITY TYPE",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.6f)
                )

                Box {
                    Card(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .clickable { expandedDropdown = true },
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, BorderGray),
                        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = SprintaDarkSurface)
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(16.dp),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                activityOptions.firstOrNull { it.first == selectedActivity }?.second ?: "ðŸƒâ€â™‚ï¸ Run",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
                                color = SprintaWhite
                            )
                            Icon(Icons.Default.ArrowDropDown, contentDescription = "Select", tint = SprintaGold)
                        }
                    }

                    DropdownMenu(
                        expanded = expandedDropdown,
                        onDismissRequest = { expandedDropdown = false }
                    ) {
                        activityOptions.forEach { (type, label) ->
                            DropdownMenuItem(
                                text = { Text(label) },
                                onClick = {
                                    selectedActivity = type
                                    expandedDropdown = false
                                }
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }

                SprintaTextField(
                    value = distanceText,
                    onValueChange = { distanceText = it },
                    label = "Distance (km)",
                    keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number)
                )

                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    SprintaTextField(
                        value = hoursText,
                        onValueChange = { hoursText = it },
                        label = "Hours",
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                        keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number)
                    )
                    SprintaTextField(
                        value = minsText,
                        onValueChange = { minsText = it },
                        label = "Minutes",
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                        keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number)
                    )
                }

                SprintaTextField(
                    value = notesText,
                    onValueChange = { notesText = it },
                    label = "Notes",
                    singleLine = false
                )
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            Button(
                onClick = {
                    val dist = distanceText.toDoubleOrNull() ?: 0.0
                    val hours = hoursText.toLongOrNull() ?: 0L
                    val mins = minsText.toLongOrNull() ?: 0L
                    val totalMins = (hours * 60L) + mins
                    val nowIso = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss'Z'", Locale.US).format(Date())

                    onSave(selectedActivity, dist, totalMins, nowIso, notesText)
                    onDismiss()
                },
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = SprintaGold, contentColor = SprintaWhite)
            ) {
                Text("Save Workout", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }
        },
        dismissButton = {
            OutlinedButton(
                onClick = onDismiss,
                border = BorderStroke(1.dp, BorderGray)
            ) {
                Text("Cancel", color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground)
            }
        }
    )
}

                                    .weight(pct.coerceAtLeast(0.01f))

                        Text("Z1 15%", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, color = Color(0xFF2196F3))
                        Text("Z2 25%", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, color = Color(0xFF4CAF50))
                        Text("Z3 35%", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, color = Color(0xFFFFEB3B))
                        Text("Z4 15%", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, color = Color(0xFFFF9800))
                        Text("Z5 10%", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, color = Color(0xFFF44336))
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        item {
            Card(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                border = BorderStroke(1.dp, BorderGray),
                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = SprintaDarkSurface)
            ) {
                Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                    Text(
                        text = "12-WEEK TRAINING PROGRESSION",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                        color = SprintaGold
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                    val points = state.weeklyVolumeKm
                    val maxVal = remember(points) { (points.maxOrNull() ?: 1f).coerceAtLeast(1f) }

                    Canvas(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .height(140.dp)
                    ) {
                        val width = size.width
                        val height = size.height
                        val stepX = width / (points.size - 1)

                        val path = Path()
                        val fillPath = Path()

                        points.forEachIndexed { i, valKm ->
                            val x = i * stepX
                            val y = height - (valKm / maxVal * height * 0.8f)

                            if (i == 0) {
                                path.moveTo(x, y)
                                fillPath.moveTo(x, height)
                                fillPath.lineTo(x, y)
                            } else {
                                val prevX = (i - 1) * stepX
                                val prevY = height - (points[i - 1] / maxVal * height * 0.8f)
                                val controlX = (prevX + x) / 2f
                                path.cubicTo(controlX, prevY, controlX, y, x, y)
                                fillPath.cubicTo(controlX, prevY, controlX, y, x, y)
                            }
                        }

                        fillPath.lineTo(width, height)
                        fillPath.close()

                        drawPath(
                            path = fillPath,
                            brush = Brush.verticalGradient(
                                colors = listOf(SprintaGold.copy(alpha = 0.4f), Color.Transparent)
                            )
                        )

                        drawPath(
                            path = path,
                            color = SprintaGold,
                            style = Stroke(width = 3.dp.toPx())
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun PredictorBlock(
    label: String,
    time: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    Column(modifier = modifier, horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
        Text(
            text = label,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
            color = SprintaWhite.copy(alpha = 0.6f)
        )
        Text(
            text = time,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
            color = SprintaWhite
        )
    }
}

package za.co.sprinta.app.ui.profile

import androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Box

                        onValueChange = vm::onBioChanged,
                        label = "Bio / Moto",
                        singleLine = false
                    )

                    SprintaTextField(
                        value = uiState.location,
                        onValueChange = vm::onLocationChanged,
                        label = "Location"
                    )
                }
            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

            PrimaryButton(
                text = if (uiState.isSaving) "Saving..." else "Save Changes",
                onClick = {
                    vm.onSaveProfile()
                    onBackClick()
                },
                enabled = !uiState.isSaving
            )

            if (uiState.isSaving) {
                CircularProgressIndicator(color = SprintaGold, modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp))
            }
        }
    }

package za.co.sprinta.app.ui.profile

import androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke
import androidx.compose.foundation.Canvas
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement

import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Spacer

import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.height

@Composable
fun ProfileScreen(
    vm: MainViewModel,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val session by vm.session.collectAsState()
    val workouts by vm.workouts.collectAsState()
    val prefs by vm.preferences.collectAsState()

    var selectedTabIndex by remember { mutableIntStateOf(0) }
    val tabTitles = listOf("Progress", "Activities")

    val userName = session?.get("name") ?: "Athlete"
    val email = session?.get("email") ?: "athlete@sprinta.app"

    val totalRuns = workouts.size
    val totalMeters = workouts.sumOf { it.distanceMeters }
    val totalSeconds = workouts.sumOf { it.durationSeconds }
    val avgPace = GeoUtils.paceSecondsPerKm(totalMeters, totalSeconds)

    val distanceKm = if (prefs.useMiles) totalMeters / 1609.344 else totalMeters / 1000.0
    val unitLabel = if (prefs.useMiles) "mi" else "km"

    Column(modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
        TabRow(
            selectedTabIndex = selectedTabIndex,
            containerColor = SprintaDarkSurface,
            contentColor = SprintaGold,
            indicator = { tabPositions ->
                TabRowDefaults.SecondaryIndicator(
                    modifier = Modifier.tabIndicatorOffset(tabPositions[selectedTabIndex]),
                    color = SprintaGold
                )
            }
        ) {
            tabTitles.forEachIndexed { index, title ->
                Tab(
                    selected = selectedTabIndex == index,
                    onClick = { selectedTabIndex = index },
                    text = {
                        Text(
                            text = title,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                            color = if (selectedTabIndex == index) SprintaGold else SprintaWhite.copy(alpha = 0.6f)
                        )
                    }
                )
            }
        }

        LazyColumn(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(18.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
        ) {
            if (selectedTabIndex == 0) {
                item {
                    Card(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
                        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, BorderGray),
                        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = SprintaDarkSurface)
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(20.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .clip(CircleShape)
                                    .background(SprintaGold)
                                    .padding(16.dp)
                            ) {
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = Icons.Default.Person,
                                    contentDescription = "Profile Avatar",
                                    tint = SprintaBlack,
                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(4.dp)
                                )
                            }
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(16.dp))
                            Column {
                                Text(
                                    text = userName,
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                    color = SprintaWhite
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = email,
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                                    color = SprintaWhite.copy(alpha = 0.6f)
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                                Text(
                                    text = "âš¡ 3-WEEK STREAK",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                    color = SprintaGold
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                item {
                    Text(
                        text = "ALL-TIME STATS",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.6f),
                        letterSpacing = 1.sp
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                    Card(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, BorderGray),
                        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = SprintaDarkSurface)
                    ) {
                        Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                            ) {
                                Text("Total Activities", color = SprintaWhite.copy(alpha = 0.7f))
                                Text("$totalRuns", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = SprintaWhite)
                            }
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                            ) {
                                Text("Total Distance", color = SprintaWhite.copy(alpha = 0.7f))
                                Text("%.2f %s".format(distanceKm, unitLabel), fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = SprintaGold)
                            }
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                            ) {
                                Text("Total Time", color = SprintaWhite.copy(alpha = 0.7f))
                                Text(GeoUtils.formatDuration(totalSeconds), fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = SprintaWhite)
                            }
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                            ) {
                                Text("Average Pace", color = SprintaWhite.copy(alpha = 0.7f))
                                Text(GeoUtils.formatPace(avgPace), fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = SprintaWhite)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                item {
                    Text(
                        text = "12-WEEK TRAINING VOLUME",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.6f),
                        letterSpacing = 1.sp
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                    Card(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, BorderGray),
                        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = SprintaDarkSurface)
                    ) {
                        Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                            val volumes = remember { listOf(12f, 18f, 25f, 30f, 22f, 15f, 35f, 28f, 40f, 32f, 45f, 50f) }
                            val maxVol = remember(volumes) { (volumes.maxOrNull() ?: 1f).coerceAtLeast(1f) }

                            Canvas(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .height(150.dp)
                            ) {
                                val width = size.width
                                val height = size.height
                                val stepX = width / (volumes.size - 1)

                                val strokePath = Path()
                                val fillPath = Path()
                                val nodePositions = mutableListOf<Offset>()

                                volumes.forEachIndexed { i, vol ->
                                    val x = i * stepX
                                    val y = height - (vol / maxVol * height * 0.8f) - 10f
                                    nodePositions.add(Offset(x, y))

                                    if (i == 0) {
                                        strokePath.moveTo(x, y)
                                        fillPath.moveTo(x, height)
                                        fillPath.lineTo(x, y)
                                    } else {
                                        val prevX = (i - 1) * stepX
                                        val prevY = height - (volumes[i - 1] / maxVol * height * 0.8f) - 10f
                                        val controlX = (prevX + x) / 2f
                                        strokePath.cubicTo(controlX, prevY, controlX, y, x, y)
                                        fillPath.cubicTo(controlX, prevY, controlX, y, x, y)
                                    }
                                }

                                fillPath.lineTo(width, height)
                                fillPath.close()

                                drawPath(
                                    path = fillPath,
                                    brush = Brush.verticalGradient(
                                        colors = listOf(
                                            SprintaGold.copy(alpha = 0.5f),
                                            Color.Transparent
                                        )
                                    )
                                )

                                drawPath(
                                    path = strokePath,
                                    color = SprintaGold,
                                    style = Stroke(width = 3.dp.toPx())
                                )

                                nodePositions.forEach { pos ->
                                    drawCircle(
                                        color = SprintaGold,
                                        radius = 5.dp.toPx(),
                                        center = pos
                                    )
                                    drawCircle(
                                        color = SprintaDarkSurface,
                                        radius = 2.dp.toPx(),
                                        center = pos
                                    )
                                }
                            }

                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = "Weekly Volume Trend ($unitLabel)",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                color = SprintaWhite.copy(alpha = 0.5f),
                                modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.CenterHorizontally)
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else {
                item {
                    Text(
                        text = "RECENT ACTIVITIES",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.6f),
                        letterSpacing = 1.sp
                    )
                }

                if (workouts.isEmpty()) {
                    item {
                        Text(
                            text = "No activities logged yet.",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                            color = SprintaWhite.copy(alpha = 0.6f)
                        )
                    }
                } else {
                    items(workouts, key = { it.workoutId }) { workout ->
                        WorkoutActivityRow(workout = workout, useMiles = prefs.useMiles)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun WorkoutActivityRow(workout: WorkoutEntity, useMiles: Boolean) {
    Card(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        border = BorderStroke(1.dp, BorderGray),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = SprintaDarkSurface)
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
            Text(
                text = "${workout.activityType} â€¢ ${GeoUtils.formatDuration(workout.durationSeconds)}",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                color = SprintaGold
            )
            Text(
                text = "Distance: ${if (useMiles) "%.2f mi".format(workout.distanceMeters / 1609.344) else "%.2f km".format(workout.distanceMeters / 1000.0)}",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                color = SprintaWhite
            )
            Text(
                text = "Pace: ${GeoUtils.formatPace(workout.averagePaceSecondsPerKm)}",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                color = SprintaWhite.copy(alpha = 0.7f)
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun SettingsScreen(
    vm: MainViewModel,
    prefs: AppPreferences,
    onEditProfileClick: () -> Unit = {},
    onUpgradeClick: () -> Unit = {},
    onAboutClick: () -> Unit = {},
    onLogoutClick: () -> Unit = {},
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    var showDeleteDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    var isAutoPauseEnabled by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }
    var isAudioCuesEnabled by remember { mutableStateOf(prefs.audioIntervalKm > 0) }
    var isDarkTheme by remember { mutableStateOf(prefs.darkMode) }
    var isPrivateProfile by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var useMetric by remember { mutableStateOf(!prefs.useMiles) }

    if (showDeleteDialog) {
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showDeleteDialog = false },
            title = {
                Text(
                    "Delete Account?",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground
                )
            },
            text = {
                Text(
                    "Are you sure you want to permanently delete your Sprinta account and saved workout data? This action cannot be undone.",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground
                )
            },
            confirmButton = {
                Button(
                    onClick = {
                        showDeleteDialog = false
                        vm.deleteAccount()
                    },
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = SprintaError, contentColor = SprintaWhite)
                ) {
                    Text("Delete Account", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                OutlinedButton(
                    onClick = { showDeleteDialog = false },
                    border = BorderStroke(1.dp, BorderGray)
                ) {
                    Text("Cancel", color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground)
                }
            }
        )
    }

    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(SprintaBlack)
            .padding(18.dp)
            .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
    ) {
        Text(
            text = "Settings",
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
            color = SprintaWhite
        )

        SettingsSectionHeader(title = "ACCOUNT & SUBSCRIPTION")
        Card(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
            border = BorderStroke(1.dp, BorderGray),
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = SprintaDarkSurface)
        ) {
            Column {
                SettingsClickableItem(
                    title = "Edit Profile",
                    subtitle = "Name, bio & location",
                    icon = Icons.Default.Person,
                    onClick = onEditProfileClick
                )
                SettingsClickableItem(
                    title = "Sprinta Pro Subscription",
                    subtitle = "Manage premium features",
                    icon = Icons.Default.Star,
                    iconTint = SprintaGold,
                    onClick = onUpgradeClick
                )
            }
        }

        SettingsSectionHeader(title = "TRACKING & SENSORS")
        Card(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
            border = BorderStroke(1.dp, BorderGray),
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = SprintaDarkSurface)
        ) {
            Column {
                SettingsToggleItem(
                    title = "Auto-Pause",
                    subtitle = "Pause recording when you stop moving",
                    icon = Icons.Default.PauseCircle,
                    isChecked = isAutoPauseEnabled,
                    onCheckedChange = { isAutoPauseEnabled = it }
                )
                SettingsToggleItem(
                    title = "Audio Cues",
                    subtitle = "Pace and distance announcements",
                    icon = Icons.Default.VolumeUp,
                    isChecked = isAudioCuesEnabled,
                    onCheckedChange = {
                        isAudioCuesEnabled = it
                        vm.setAudioInterval(if (it) 1 else 0)
                    }
                )

@Composable
fun SettingsSectionHeader(title: String) {
    Text(
        text = title,
        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
        color = SprintaGold,
        letterSpacing = 1.sp,
        modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp, bottom = 4.dp)
    )
}

@Composable
fun SettingsToggleItem(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    icon: ImageVector,
    isChecked: Boolean,
    onCheckedChange: (Boolean) -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    Row(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clickable { onCheckedChange(!isChecked) }
            .padding(16.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Icon(
            imageVector = icon,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = SprintaGold,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(end = 16.dp)
        )
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(
                text = title,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold),
                color = SprintaWhite
            )
            Text(
                text = subtitle,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                color = SprintaWhite.copy(alpha = 0.6f)
            )
        }
        Switch(
            checked = isChecked,
            onCheckedChange = onCheckedChange,
            colors = SwitchDefaults.colors(
                checkedThumbColor = SprintaBlack,
                checkedTrackColor = SprintaGold,
                uncheckedThumbColor = SprintaWhite.copy(alpha = 0.6f),
                uncheckedTrackColor = BorderGray
            )
        )
    }
}

@Composable
fun SettingsClickableItem(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String? = null,
    icon: ImageVector,
    iconTint: Color = SprintaGold,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    Row(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clickable { onClick() }
            .padding(16.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Icon(
            imageVector = icon,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = iconTint,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(end = 16.dp)
        )
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(
                text = title,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold),
                color = SprintaWhite
            )
            if (subtitle != null) {
                Text(
                    text = subtitle,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                    color = SprintaWhite.copy(alpha = 0.6f)
                )
            }
        }
        Icon(
            imageVector = Icons.Default.ChevronRight,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = SprintaWhite.copy(alpha = 0.4f)
        )
    }
}

@Composable
fun LeaderboardEntryCard(
    entry: LeaderboardEntry,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val rankBadgeColor = when (entry.rank) {
        1 -> SprintaGold
        2 -> Color(0xFFC0C0C0)
        3 -> Color(0xFFCD7F32)
        else -> BorderGray
    }

    val cardBorder = if (entry.isCurrentUser) {
        BorderStroke(2.dp, SprintaGold)
    } else {
        BorderStroke(1.dp, BorderGray)
    }

    Card(
        modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        border = cardBorder,
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
            containerColor = if (entry.isCurrentUser) SprintaDarkSurface.copy(alpha = 0.95f) else SprintaDarkSurface
        )
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(16.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(36.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(rankBadgeColor),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                if (entry.rank <= 3) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Default.EmojiEvents,
                        contentDescription = "Rank",
                        tint = SprintaBlack,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                    )
                } else {
                    Text(
                        text = "#${entry.rank}",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                        color = SprintaWhite
                    )
                }
            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(16.dp))

            Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Text(
                    text = entry.userName + if (entry.isCurrentUser) " (You)" else "",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                    color = if (entry.isCurrentUser) SprintaGold else SprintaWhite
                )
                Text(
                    text = "${entry.totalWorkouts} activities this week",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                    color = SprintaWhite.copy(alpha = 0.6f)
                )
            }

            Text(
                text = "%.1f km".format(entry.weeklyDistanceKm),
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                color = SprintaGold
            )
        }
    }
}
