package za.co.sprinta.app

import android.app.Activity
import android.app.Application
import android.os.Bundle
import androidx.lifecycle.DefaultLifecycleObserver
import androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleOwner
import androidx.lifecycle.ProcessLifecycleOwner
import com.google.android.gms.ads.AdRequest
import com.google.android.gms.ads.appopen.AppOpenAd
import za.co.sprinta.app.ads.AdUnitIds

class SprintaApplication : Application(), Application.ActivityLifecycleCallbacks, DefaultLifecycleObserver {

    private var currentActivity: Activity? = null
    private var appOpenAd: AppOpenAd? = null
    private var isAdLoading = false

    override fun onCreate() {
        super<Application>.onCreate()
        AppGraph.init(this)
        registerActivityLifecycleCallbacks(this)
        ProcessLifecycleOwner.get().lifecycle.addObserver(this)
    }

    override fun onStart(owner: LifecycleOwner) {
        super.onStart(owner)
        showAppOpenAdIfAvailable()
    }

    private fun showAppOpenAdIfAvailable() {
        val activity = currentActivity ?: return

        if (appOpenAd != null) {
            appOpenAd?.show(activity)
            appOpenAd = null
            fetchAppOpenAd()
        } else {
            fetchAppOpenAd()
        }
    }

    private fun fetchAppOpenAd() {
        if (isAdLoading || appOpenAd != null) return

        isAdLoading = true
        val request = AdRequest.Builder().build()
        AppOpenAd.load(
            this,
            AdUnitIds.APP_OPEN,
            request,
            object : AppOpenAd.AppOpenAdLoadCallback() {
                override fun onAdLoaded(ad: AppOpenAd) {
                    appOpenAd = ad
                    isAdLoading = false
                }

                override fun onAdFailedToLoad(loadAdError: com.google.android.gms.ads.LoadAdError) {
                    isAdLoading = false
                }
            }
        )
    }

    override fun onActivityCreated(activity: Activity, savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {}
    override fun onActivityStarted(activity: Activity) { currentActivity = activity }
    override fun onActivityResumed(activity: Activity) { currentActivity = activity }
    override fun onActivityPaused(activity: Activity) {}
    override fun onActivityStopped(activity: Activity) {}
    override fun onActivitySaveInstanceState(activity: Activity, outState: Bundle) {}
    override fun onActivityDestroyed(activity: Activity) {
        if (currentActivity == activity) {
            currentActivity = null
        }
    }
}

package za.co.sprinta.app

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import za.co.sprinta.app.util.GeoUtils

class GeoUtilsTest {
    @Test fun paceCalculationIsCorrect() {
        assertEquals(300.0, GeoUtils.paceSecondsPerKm(5000.0, 1500L), 0.001)
    }

    @Test fun haversineDistanceIsPositive() {
        val distance = GeoUtils.haversineMeters(-33.9249, 18.4241, -33.9250, 18.4251)
        assertTrue(distance > 0)
    }

    @Test fun durationFormattingIsCorrect() {
        assertEquals("05:32", GeoUtils.formatDuration(332))
    }
}


=== C:\Users\dskip\Downloads\SPRINTA_POE_PART2_SOURCE\SPRINTA_POE_PART2\app\src\test\java\za\co\sprinta\app\PersonalRecordUtilsTest.kt ===

package za.co.sprinta.app

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test
import za.co.sprinta.app.data.local.PersonalRecordEntity
import za.co.sprinta.app.data.local.WorkoutEntity
import za.co.sprinta.app.util.PersonalRecordUtils

class PersonalRecordUtilsTest {
    private fun workout(pace: Double) = WorkoutEntity(
        workoutId = "run-1",
        userId = "user-1",
        distanceMeters = 5000.0,
        durationSeconds = (pace * 5).toLong(),
        averagePaceSecondsPerKm = pace,
        startTimeIso = "2026-09-21T10:00:00Z",
        totalElevation = 20.0,
        notes = "",
        routePointsJson = "[]",
        isSynced = false
    )

    @Test fun fasterFiveKmCreatesRecord() {
        val current = PersonalRecordEntity(
            key = "user-1-5K",
            userId = "user-1",
            recordType = "5K",
            valueSecondsPerKm = 360.0,
            workoutId = "old",
            achievedAtIso = "2026-09-01T10:00:00Z"
        )

        val records = PersonalRecordUtils.detectNewRecords(workout(340.0), listOf(current))
        assertEquals(1, records.count { it.type == "5K" })
    }
}

package za.co.sprinta.app

import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import za.co.sprinta.app.util.Validators

class ValidatorsTest {
    @Test fun validEmailIsAccepted() = assertTrue(Validators.isValidEmail("runner@example.com"))
    @Test fun invalidEmailIsRejected() = assertFalse(Validators.isValidEmail("runner@"))
    @Test fun strongPasswordIsAccepted() = assertTrue(Validators.isValidPassword("Password@123"))
    @Test fun weakPasswordIsRejected() = assertFalse(Validators.isValidPassword("password"))
}
